    rgba for each line  
    r -> Line style  
    ---> Lower 3 bits for line style

    ---> Upper 5 bits for line width (so max width of 32px (1 is added to value))

  float lineStyle = floor(lineStyleAndWidth + 0.5 - lineWidth * 8.);
  lineWidth += 1.0; // to make range 1-32 instead of 0-31
  lineWidth *= (1.0 - fade);

  vec3 pos = position.xyz;
  pos.z += heightOffset; // lines slightly above ground
  vec3 normal = normal_index.xyz;

  ndc.x += 100. / renderWidth; // 1, not 2 as want half a pixel | Precision issue,

  inv.xyz /= inv.w;                                          // | by 100 then divide
